# NetOps query catalog

Shared [identity, profiles, typed slots and result states](normalization.md).
Normalized tables and bounded endpoint correlation use these templates and permissions: [switch_read / locate_endpoint](tools.md#normalized-tables-switch_read). No separate command catalogue is maintained.

This file is generated by `tests/support/render_query_catalog_docs.py` from the runtime query authority and `docs/query-sources.json`. Do not edit it manually.

It records 1107 exact named query templates across 11 canonical profiles. Parameters are accepted only through the listed inventory-bound type. `high-volume: yes` is a maintainer advisory that the fixed command returns a variable collection or history known or conservatively expected to require continuation pages. It does not change authorization, opt-in, rate accounting, timeout, snapshot or cache behavior, or the 2,000,000-byte capture cap. `high-volume: no` is not a promise that output is small, cheap, or bounded.

The Phase-1 catalog intentionally excludes running, startup, full, backup, and exported configuration (a few templates read one fixed configuration section); arbitrary CLI; log following or export beyond the fixed log-view templates listed below; debug activation or modification; support bundles; packet capture; file display; shells; and every write or lifecycle action.

Source links establish reviewed syntax and purpose. They do not prove support, output shape, read-only AAA behavior, or transmitted bytes on a particular target. A source named without a link is a vendor guide that is only reachable behind a vendor login; its row carries the date the syntax was run on a device instead. See the [vendor audit index](vendor-cli-references.md) for limitations and exclusions.

## `linux`

Baseline: NetOps Linux command contract

| Profile | Query | Exact command template | Parameter -> inventory.kind | High-volume | Description | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>linux</code> | <code>addresses</code> | <code>ip -brief address</code> | none | yes | Summarize interface addresses. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>bridge_fdb</code> | <code>bridge fdb show</code> | none | yes | Show the bridge forwarding database. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>filesystems</code> | <code>df -h</code> | none | no | Show mounted filesystem usage. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>hostname</code> | <code>hostname</code> | none | no | Show the system hostname. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>interface_addresses</code> | <code>ip -brief address show dev {interface}</code> | <code>interface -&gt; interfaces.interface</code> | no | Show addresses on one enrolled interface. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>interface_link</code> | <code>ip -details link show dev {interface}</code> | <code>interface -&gt; interfaces.interface</code> | no | Show detailed state for one enrolled interface. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>kernel</code> | <code>uname -a</code> | none | no | Show kernel and architecture details. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>links</code> | <code>ip -brief link</code> | none | yes | Summarize network link state. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>memory</code> | <code>free -m</code> | none | no | Show memory usage in MiB. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>neighbors</code> | <code>ip neigh show</code> | none | yes | Show the IP neighbor table. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>routes</code> | <code>ip route show</code> | none | yes | Show the IPv4 routing table. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>running_services</code> | <code>systemctl --no-pager --state=running --type=service</code> | none | yes | Show running systemd services. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>service_logs_recent</code> | <code>journalctl --no-pager -u {service} --since -1h</code> | <code>service -&gt; services.service</code> | yes | Show the last hour of logs for one enrolled service. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>service_status</code> | <code>systemctl --no-pager --full status {service}</code> | <code>service -&gt; services.service</code> | no | Show status for one enrolled systemd service. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>sockets</code> | <code>ss -lntup</code> | none | yes | Show listening TCP and UDP sockets. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |
| <code>linux</code> | <code>uptime</code> | <code>uptime</code> | none | no | Show uptime and load averages. | Project contract; upstream source audit pending. |

| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>switch_mac_limit</code> | <code>diagnose switch-controller switch-info mac-limit-violations interface {managed_switch} {port}</code> | <code>managed_switch -&gt; managed_switches.managed_switch_serial</code><br><code>port -&gt; managed_switch_ports.managed_switch_port</code> | no | Show MAC learning-limit violations reported for one enrolled managed-switch port; an empty table is meaningful only when the port sets learning-limit above 0. | [F-CLI-768](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/7.6.8/cli-reference)<br>[F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |
| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>switch_mclag_consistency</code> | <code>diagnose switch-controller switch-info mclag peer-consistency-check {managed_switch} {mclag_trunk}</code> | <code>managed_switch -&gt; managed_switches.managed_switch_serial</code><br><code>mclag_trunk -&gt; managed_switch_trunks.managed_switch_trunk</code> | no | Compare one enrolled MCLAG trunk with the MCLAG peer (configuration checks, LAG and STP state) and list inactive local or peer members; it does not show link state. | [F-CLI-768](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/7.6.8/cli-reference)<br>[F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |
| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>switch_mclag_icl</code> | <code>diagnose switch-controller switch-info mclag icl {managed_switch}</code> | <code>managed_switch -&gt; managed_switches.managed_switch_serial</code> | no | Show the MCLAG inter-chassis link of one enrolled managed switch; this is only configuration (ICL ports, peer serial), read ICL link health with switch_trunk_status; unsupported switches report not_supported. | [F-CLI-768](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/7.6.8/cli-reference)<br>[F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |

| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>system_status</code> | <code>get system status</code> | none | no | Show FortiOS version, system identity, and operating status. | [F-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/7.6.4/cli-reference)<br>[F-CLI-80](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.0/cli-reference)<br>[F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference)<br>[F-CHEAT-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/7.6.0/cli-troubleshooting-cheat-sheet/420966/cli-troubleshooting-cheat-sheet)<br>[F-CHEAT-80](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.0/cli-troubleshooting-cheat-sheet/420966/cli-troubleshooting-cheat-sheet)<br>[F-CHEAT-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-troubleshooting-cheat-sheet) |

| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>wifi_controller_status</code> | <code>get wireless-controller status</code> | none | no | Show wireless controller session and client counters. | [F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference)<br>[F-WIFI-STATUS-600](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/6.0.0/cli-reference/681997/wireless-controller-status) |
| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>wifi_events</code> | <code>execute log filter category event; execute log filter field subtype wireless; execute log filter view-lines {lines}; execute log display</code> | <code>lines -&gt; literal.wifi_log_lines</code><br><code>mac_literal -&gt; literal.wifi_mac_literal</code> | yes | Show the newest wireless event log records (client connect, disconnect and failure events, rogue access point events) from one SSH session with a session-local display filter (no persistent configuration write); triage prefers an enrolled read-only REST log GET. Lines is 1 to 200 and the optional mac_literal keeps only records that contain that lower-case MAC address, applied by the Helper to the redacted records of the returned lines only. | [F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |

| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>wifi_rf_analysis</code> | <code>get wireless-controller rf-analysis {wtp_id}</code> | <code>wtp_id -&gt; wtp_ids.fortios_wtp_id</code> | yes | Show per-channel RF analysis (rssi total, rf score, overlapping and interfering access points, channel utilisation); wtp_id narrows the output to one access point listed in wtp_ids or returned by an earlier wifi_ap_status for this target. | [F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |

| <code>fortinet</code> | <code>wifi_vap_status</code> | <code>get wireless-controller vap-status</code> | none | no | Show wireless virtual access points (SSIDs) with state and station counts. | [F-CLI-802](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortigate/8.0.2/cli-reference) |

Baseline: FortiADC 7.6.7

| Profile | Query | Exact command template | Parameter -> inventory.kind | High-volume | Description | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>arp_table</code> | <code>diagnose netlink neighbor list</code> | none | yes | Show the IPv4 neighbor (ARP) table with hardware addresses and interfaces. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference)<br>[F-ADC-DIAGNOSE-NETLINK-NEIGHBOR-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference/354318/diagnose-netlink-neighbor-neighbor6) |
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>autoupdate_contract</code> | <code>execute autoupdate contract</code> | none | no | Show the licensed update service contracts with serial number, contract expiry per service, support level, and support type descriptions. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference)<br>[F-ADC-EXECUTE-AUTOUPDATE-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference/244507/execute-autoupdate) |
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>bgp_neighbors</code> | <code>get router info bgp neighbors</code> | none | yes | Show detailed BGP neighbor state, timers, capabilities, and message counters for session troubleshooting. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference)<br>[F-ADC-GET-ROUTER-INFO-BGP-NEIGHBORS-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference/756515/get-router-info-bgp-neighbors) |
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>bgp_summary</code> | <code>get router info bgp summary</code> | none | no | Show BGP neighbor state, uptime, and received-prefix counts for routing-session diagnosis. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference)<br>[F-ADC-GET-ROUTER-INFO-BGP-SUMMARY-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference/858752/get-router-info-bgp-summary) |
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>certificate_inventory</code> | <code>get system certificate local</code> | none | no | Inventory local, intermediate CA and CA certificates, expiry and SSL references from pinned FortiADC HTTPS, with ACME status from SSH. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference) |
| <code>fortiadc</code> | <code>cpu_info</code> | <code>diagnose hardware get sysinfo cpu</code> | none | yes | Show the per-processor CPU model, flags, and cache details as listed in the Linux cpuinfo format. | [F-ADC-CLI-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference)<br>[F-ADC-DIAGNOSE-HARDWARE-SYSINFO-76](https://docs.fortinet.com/document/fortiadc/7.6.7/cli-reference/106768/diagnose-hardware-sysinfo) |
